domains.bbob_unconstrained.utils.format_input_dict\n        Expected keys: 'problem_id','dimension','instance'\n        log_fn: logging function\n        mode: 'train' for training DQN (updates # \u4ece problem_id \u5b57\u7b26\u4e32\u4e2d\u63d0\u53d6 function \u7f16\u53f7\uff08\u4f8b\u5982 f1_i1 -> 1\uff09\n    problem_id_str = inputs.get('problem_id', 'f1_i1')\n    problem_id = int(str(problem_id_str).replace('f', '').split('_')[0])\n\n    suite = cocoex.Suite('bbob', '', f'dimensions: {dim} instance_indices:{inst} function_indices: {problem_id}')\n    # pick the matching problem from the suite\n    problem = next(iter(suite))\n\n    # run DE algorithm using DEOptimizer to get stats\n    try:\n        de_opt = ec_mod.DEOptimizer(problem.dimension, getattr(problem,    \"\"\"Called at the start of optimization for a new problem/run.\"\"\"\n    29\t\n    30\t    @abstractmethod\n    31\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    32\t observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    43\t        return dict(self.params)\n    44\t\n    45\t\n    46\tclass DummyHeuristicController(ParamController):\n    47\t    \"\"\"A tiny        self.w_start = w_start\n    51\t        self.w_end = w_end\n    52\t        self.decay_iters = decay_iters\n    53\t\n    54\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    55\t        self.it = 0\n    56\t\n    57\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    58\t        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n    59\t        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max(1, self.decay_iters))\n    60\t        w = self.w_start * (1 - t) + self.w_end * t\n    61\t        # Encourage exploration early, exploitation later\n    62\t        c1 = 1.8 * (1.0 - 0.5 * t)\n    63\t        c2 = 1.4 + 0.4 * t\n    64\t        # Also support DE-style parameters for multi-objective DE optimizers\n    65\t        F = 0.9 - 0.5 * t  # Large mutation early, refined late\n    66\t        CR = 0.9 - 0.4 * t # More crossover early for diversity\n    67\t        return {'w': w, 'c1': c1, 'c2': c2, 'F': F, 'CR': CR}\n    68\t\n    69\t\n    70\tclass AdaptiveDEController(ParamController):\n    71\t    \"\"\"Adaptive controller for DE/JADE algorithms with epsilon-constrained support.\n    72\t\n    73\t    - Early iterations: high F (exploration), high CR (diversity),\n    74\t      use 'rand/1' strategy to explore broadly.\n    75\t    - Late iterations: lower F (exploitation), moderate CR (retain\n    76\t      good building blocks), switch to 'jade' for fast convergence.\n    77\t    - Uses stagnation detection to temporarily boost F/CR.\n    78\t    - Provides a decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained handling,\n    79\t      allowing the optimizer to explore mildly infeasible regions early and\n    80\t      enforcing feasibility as the budget is consumed.\n    81\t    - If constraint violations exploitation\n   108\t        F = self.f_start * (1 - t) + self.f_end * t\n   109\t        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\n   110\t\n   111\t        # Detect stagnation: no improvement 131\t\n   132\t        # Decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained DE.\n   133\t        # Use log-scale interpolation for smooth transition.\n   134\t        log_eps_start = float(np_log(self.epsilon_start))\n   135\t        log_eps_end = float(np_log(self.epsilon_end))\n   136\t        log_eps = log_eps_start + t * (log_eps_end - log_eps_start)\n   137\t        epsilon = float(np_exp(log_eps))\n   138\t\n   139\t        # If the current best is still infeasible late in the run, relax epsilon\n   140\t        # slightly so the algorithm is not forced into a tiny feasible region.\n   141\t        current_gbest_cv = observation.get('gbest_cv', None)\n   142\t        if current_gbest_cv is not None and t > 0.5 and current_gbest_cv > epsilon:\n   143\t Initialize population - uniform random initialization\\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\\n        \\n        # Evaluate initial population\\n        evals = 0\\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\\n        \\n        for i in range(pop_size):\\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                break\\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\\n            evals += 1\\n\\n        # Track best\\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\\n        \\n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\\n\\n        # Main loop\\n        iteration = 0\\n        done = False\\n        \\n        while not done:\\n            iteration += 1\\n            params = dict(self.params)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\\n                    params.update(step_params)\\n            \\n            F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\\n            CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\\n            \\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\\n            \\n            # Generate trial vectors\\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                # Tournament selection (tournsize=3, does NOT exclude current individual)\\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\\n                if len(candidates) < 3:\\n                    indices = list(range(pop_size))\\n                else:\\n                    indices = rng.choice(candidates, size=3, replace=False)\\n                \\n                a, b, c = pop[indices[0]], pop[indices[1]], pop[indices[2]]\\n                \\n                # Mutation and crossover\\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\\n                for j in range(self.dim):\\n                    if rng.random() < CR or j == j_rand:\\n                        new_pop[i, j] = a[j] + F * (b[j] - c[j])\\n                \\n                # Boundary handling: clip\\n                new_pop[i] = np.clip(new_pop[i], lb, ub)\\n            \\n            # Evaluate trial vectors\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\\n                evals += 1\\n                \\n                # Greedy selection\\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\\n            \\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n            \\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n            \\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n\\n        self.stats = {\\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\\n            'evals': int(evals),\\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\\n        }\\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\",\n            \"new_str\": \"    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\\n        if self.controller is not None:\\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\\n\\n        # Use the configured swarm size (or a sensible default) as the initial\\n        # population size for the L-SHADE style optimizer.\\n        init_pop_size = max(4, int(self.pop_size))\\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\\n        \\n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\\n\\n        # L-SHADE / JADE control parameters\\n        H = 10                     # success-history memory size\\n        min_pop_size = max(4, min(init_pop_size, 4))\\n\\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (init_pop_size, self.dim))\\n        \\n        # Evaluate initial population\\n        evals = 0\\n        fitness = np.full(init_pop_size, np.inf)\\n        \\n        for i in range(init_pop_size):\\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                break\\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\\n            evals += 1\\n\\n        # Track best\\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\\n        \\n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\\n\\n        # External archive for JADE/L-SHADE current-to-pbest mutation\\n        archive = np.empty((0, self.dim))\\n        archive_fitness = np.empty(0)\\n\\n        # Success-history memory for F and CR adaptation\\n        M_F = np.full(H, 0.5)\\n        M_CR = np.full(H, 0.5)\\n        memory_index = 0\\n\\n        # Main loop\\n        iteration = 0\\n        done = False\\n        \\n        while not done:\\n            iteration += 1\\n            params = dict(self.params)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\\n                    params.update(step_params)\\n            \\n            # Controller provides base F/CR. If absent, L-SHADE success-history\\n            # adaptation is the default.\\n            base_F = float(np.clip(params.get('F', 0.5), 1e-10, 1.0))\\n            base_CR = float(np.clip(params.get('CR', 0.5), 0.0, 1.0))\\n            p = float(np.clip(params.get('pbest_ratio', 0.15), 0.05, 0.5))\\n            archive_size_factor = float(max(1.0, params.get('archive_ratio', 2.5)))\\n\\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\\n            current_pop_size = pop.shape[0]\\n\\n            new_pop = np.zeros_like(pop)\\n            new_fitness = np.full(current_pop_size, np.inf)\\n\\n            # Indices of the p-best individuals, used in current-to-pbest mutation\\n            p_best_count = max(2, int(current_pop_size * p))\\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\\n            p_best_indices = set(sorted_idx[:p_best_count].tolist())\\n\\n            S_F = []\\n            S_CR = []\\n            S_delta = []\\n\\n            for i in range(current_pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n\\n                # Generate F_i and CR_i from the success-history memory.\\n                # Use controller-provided values occasionally for exploration of\\n                # the parameter space.\\n                if rng.random() < 0.1:\\n                    F_i = base_F\\n                    CR_i = base_CR\\n                else:\\n                    r = int(rng.randint(0, H))\\n                    F_i = float(np.clip(M_F[r] + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy(), 0.0, 1.0))\\n                    CR_i = float(np.clip(M_CR[r] + 0.1 * rng.randn(), 0.0, 1.0))\\n                    if F_i < 1e-8:\\n                        F_i = float(base_F)\\n\\n                # Select a p-best individual distinct from the current one\\n                if i in p_best_indices and len(p_best_indices) > 1:\\n                    pbest_candidates = [idx for idx in p_best_indices if idx != i]\\n                    pbest_idx = int(pbest_candidates[rng.randint(0, len(pbest_candidates))])\\n                else:\\n                    pbest_idx = int(sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, p_best_count)])\\n\\n                # Select r1 (distinct from i and pbest)\\n                candidates_r1 = [idx for idx in range(current_pop_size) if idx != i and idx != pbest_idx]\\n                if len(candidates_r1) == 0:\\n                    r1 = i\\n                else:\\n                    r1 = int(candidates_r1[rng.randint(0, len(candidates_r1))])\\n\\n                # Select r2 from the population or the external archive\\n                if archive.shape[0] > 0:\\n                    combined_pop = np.vstack([pop, archive])\\n                    combined_fit = np.concatenate([fitness, archive_fitness])\\n                else:\\n                    combined_pop = pop\\n                    combined_fit = fitness\\n\\n                available = [idx for idx in range(combined_pop.shape[0])\\n                             if idx >= current_pop_size or (idx != i and idx != pbest_idx and idx != r1)]\\n                if len(available) == 0:\\n                    available = [i, pbest_idx, r1]\\n                r2 = int(available[rng.randint(0, len(available))])\\n\\n                x_i = pop[i]\\n                x_pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\\n                x_r1 = pop[r1]\\n                x_r2 = combined_pop[r2]\\n\\n                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\\n                v = x_i + F_i * (x_pbest - x_i) + F_i * (x_r1 - x_r2)\\n\\n                # Binomial crossover\\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\\n                u = np.copy(x_i)\\n                for j in range(self.dim):\\n                    if rng.random() < CR_i or j == j_rand:\\n                        u[j] = v[j]\\n\\n                # Boundary handling: midpoint repair toward parent or random reset\\n                for j in range(self.dim):\\n                    if u[j] < lb[j] or u[j] > ub[j]:\\n                        if rng.random() < 0.5:\\n                            u[j] = (lb[j] + x_i[j]) / 2.0 if u[j] < lb[j] else (ub[j] + x_i[j]) / 2.0\\n                        else:\\n                            u[j] = rng.uniform(lb[j], ub[j])\\n\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, u)\\n                evals += 1\\n\\n                # Greedy selection with success-history update\\n                if trial_f <= fitness[i]:\\n                    new_pop[i] = u\\n                    new_fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    S_F.append(F_i)\\n                    S_CR.append(CR_i)\\n                    S_delta.append(abs(fitness[i] - trial_f) + 1e-12)\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = u.copy()\\n                    # Store the replaced parent in the external archive\\n                    if archive.shape[0] < int(archive_size_factor * current_pop_size):\\n                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i]])\\n                        archive_fitness = np.append(archive_fitness, fitness[i])\\n                else:\\n                    new_pop[i] = pop[i]\\n                    new_fitness[i] = fitness[i]\\n\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n\\n            # If the budget was exhausted inside the generation, keep the old\\n            # individuals for the remaining slots.\\n            for i in range(current_pop_size):\\n                if new_fitness[i] == np.inf and fitness[i] != np.inf:\\n                    new_pop[i] = pop[i]\\n                    new_fitness[i] = fitness[i]\\n\\n            pop = new_pop.copy()\\n            fitness = new_fitness.copy()\\n\\n            # L-SHADE linear population size reduction\\n            if evals < self.max_evals:\\n                remaining_frac = 1.0 - evals / self.max_evals\\n                target_pop_size = int(round(min_pop_size + (init_pop_size - min_pop_size) * remaining_frac))\\n                target_pop_size = max(min_pop_size, target_pop_size)\\n                if target_pop_size < current_pop_size:\\n                    keep_idx = np.argsort(fitness)[:target_pop_size]\\n                    pop = pop[keep_idx].copy()\\n                    fitness = fitness[keep_idx].copy()\\n                    max_archive = int(archive_size_factor * pop.shape[0])\\n                    if archive.shape[0] > max_archive:\\n                        if max_archive > 0:\\n                            archive = archive[-max_archive:].copy()\\n                            archive_fitness = archive_fitness[-max_archive:].copy()\\n                        else:\\n                            archive = np.empty((0, self.dim))\\n                            archive_fitness = np.empty(0)\\n\\n            # Update F/CR success-history memory using successful parameters\\n            if len(S_F) > 0:\\n                SF = np.asarray(S_F, dtype=float)\\n                SCR = np.asarray(S_CR, dtype=float)\\n                delta = np.asarray(S_delta, dtype=float)\\n\\n                weights = delta / np.sum(delta)\\n                denom = np.sum(weights * SF)\\n                if denom > 0:\\n                    w_lehmer_F = float(np.sum(weights * SF * SF) / denom)\\n                else:\\n                    w_lehmer_F = float(np.mean(SF))\\n                w_mean_CR = float(np.sum(weights * SCR))\\n\\n                M_F[memory_index] = float(np.clip(w_lehmer_F, 0.0, 1.0))\\n                M_CR[memory_index] = float(np.clip(w_mean_CR, 0.0, 1.0))\\n                memory_index = (memory_index + 1) % H\\n\\n            # Compute a more informative reward: relative fitness improvement\\n            if prev_gbest_f > gbest_f + 1e-12:\\n                improved = float((prev_gbest_f - gbest_f) / max(1.0, abs(prev_gbest_f)))\\n            else:\\n                improved = 0.0\\n\\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n            \\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n\\n        self.stats = {\\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\\n            'evals': int(evals),\\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\\n        }\\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n        }\n    }\n</json>",
    "text": "<json>\n    {\n        \"tool_name\": \"editor\",\n        \"tool_input\": {\n            \"command\": \"str_replace\",\n            \"path\": \"/hada/metabbo/ec_algorithm.py\",\n            \"old_str\": \"    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\\n        if self.controller is not None:\\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\\n\\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\\n        \\n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\\n\\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\\n        \\n        # Evaluate initial population\\n        evals = 0\\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\\n        \\n        for i in range(pop_size):\\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                break\\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\\n            evals += 1\\n\\n        # Track best\\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\\n        \\n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\\n\\n        # Main loop\\n        iteration = 0\\n        done = False\\n        \\n        while not done:\\n            iteration += 1\\n            params = dict(self.params)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\\n                    params.update(step_params)\\n            \\n            F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\\n            CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\\n            \\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\\n            \\n            # Generate trial vectors\\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                # Tournament selection (tournsize=3, does NOT exclude current individual)\\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\\n                if len(candidates) < 3:\\n                    indices = list(range(pop_size))\\n                else:\\n                    indices = rng.choice(candidates, size=3, replace=False)\\n                \\n                a, b, c = pop[indices[0]], pop[indices[1]], pop[indices[2]]\\n                \\n                # Mutation and crossover\\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\\n                for j in range(self.dim):\\n                    if rng.random() < CR or j == j_rand:\\n                        new_pop[i, j] = a[j] + F * (b[j] - c[j])\\n                \\n                # Boundary handling: clip\\n                new_pop[i] = np.clip(new_pop[i], lb, ub)\\n            \\n            # Evaluate trial vectors\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\\n                evals += 1\\n                \\n                # Greedy selection\\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\\n            \\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n            \\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n            \\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n\\n        self.stats = {\\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\\n            'evals': int(evals),\\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\\n        }\\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\",\n            \"new_str\": \"    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\\n        if self.controller is not None:\\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\\n\\n        # Use the configured swarm size (or a sensible default) as the initial\\n        # population size for the L-SHADE style optimizer.\\n        init_pop_size = max(4, int(self.pop_size))\\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\\n        \\n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\\n\\n        # L-SHADE / JADE control parameters\\n        H = 10                     # success-history memory size\\n        min_pop_size = max(4, min(init_pop_size, 4))\\n\\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (init_pop_size, self.dim))\\n        \\n        # Evaluate initial population\\n        evals = 0\\n        fitness = np.full(init_pop_size, np.inf)\\n        \\n        for i in range(init_pop_size):\\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                break\\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\\n            evals += 1\\n\\n        # Track best\\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\\n        \\n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\\n\\n        # External archive for JADE/L-SHADE current-to-pbest mutation\\n        archive = np.empty((0, self.dim))\\n        archive_fitness = np.empty(0)\\n\\n        # Success-history memory for F and CR adaptation\\n        M_F = np.full(H, 0.5)\\n        M_CR = np.full(H, 0.5)\\n        memory_index = 0\\n\\n        # Main loop\\n        iteration = 0\\n        done = False\\n        \\n        while not done:\\n            iteration += 1\\n            params = dict(self.params)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\\n                    params.update(step_params)\\n            \\n            # Controller provides base F/CR. If absent, L-SHADE success-history\\n            # adaptation is the default.\\n            base_F = float(np.clip(params.get('F', 0.5), 1e-10, 1.0))\\n            base_CR = float(np.clip(params.get('CR', 0.5), 0.0, 1.0))\\n            p = float(np.clip(params.get('pbest_ratio', 0.15), 0.05, 0.5))\\n            archive_size_factor = float(max(1.0, params.get('archive_ratio', 2.5)))\\n\\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\\n            current_pop_size = pop.shape[0]\\n\\n            new_pop = np.zeros_like(pop)\\n            new_fitness = np.full(current_pop_size, np.inf)\\n\\n            # Indices of the p-best individuals, used in current-to-pbest mutation\\n            p_best_count = max(2, int(current_pop_size * p))\\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\\n            p_best_indices = set(sorted_idx[:p_best_count].tolist())\\n\\n            S_F = []\\n            S_CR = []\\n            S_delta = []\\n\\n            for i in range(current_pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n\\n                # Generate F_i and CR_i from the success-history memory.\\n                # Use controller-provided values occasionally for exploration of\\n                # the parameter space.\\n                if rng.random() < 0.1:\\n                    F_i = base_F\\n                    CR_i = base_CR\\n                else:\\n                    r = int(rng.randint(0, H))\\n                    F_i = float(np.clip(M_F[r] + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy(), 0.0, 1.0))\\n                    CR_i = float(np.clip(M_CR[r] + 0.1 * rng.randn(), 0.0, 1.0))\\n                    if F_i < 1e-8:\\n                        F_i = float(base_F)\\n\\n                # Select a p-best individual distinct from the current one\\n                if i in p_best_indices and len(p_best_indices) > 1:\\n                    pbest_candidates = [idx for idx in p_best_indices if idx != i]\\n                    pbest_idx = int(pbest_candidates[rng.randint(0, len(pbest_candidates))])\\n                else:\\n                    pbest_idx = int(sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, p_best_count)])\\n\\n                # Select r1 (distinct from i and pbest)\\n                candidates_r1 = [idx for idx in range(current_pop_size) if idx != i and idx != pbest_idx]\\n                if len(candidates_r1) == 0:\\n                    r1 = i\\n                else:\\n                    r1 = int(candidates_r1[rng.randint(0, len(candidates_r1))])\\n\\n                # Select r2 from the population or the external archive\\n                if archive.shape[0] > 0:\\n                    combined_pop = np.vstack([pop, archive])\\n                    combined_fit = np.concatenate([fitness, archive_fitness])\\n                else:\\n                    combined_pop = pop\\n                    combined_fit = fitness\\n\\n                available = [idx for idx in range(combined_pop.shape[0])\\n                             if idx >= current_pop_size or (idx != i and idx != pbest_idx and idx != r1)]\\n                if len(available) == 0:\\n                    available = [i, pbest_idx, r1]\\n                r2 = int(available[rng.randint(0, len(available))])\\n\\n                x_i = pop[i]\\n                x_pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\\n                x_r1 = pop[r1]\\n                x_r2 = combined_pop[r2]\\n\\n                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\\n                v = x_i + F_i * (x_pbest - x_i) + F_i * (x_r1 - x_r2)\\n\\n                # Binomial crossover\\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\\n                u = np.copy(x_i)\\n                for j in range(self.dim):\\n                    if rng.random() < CR_i or j == j_rand:\\n                        u[j] = v[j]\\n\\n                # Boundary handling: midpoint repair toward parent or random reset\\n                for j in range(self.dim):\\n                    if u[j] < lb[j] or u[j] > ub[j]:\\n                        if rng.random() < 0.5:\\n                            u[j] = (lb[j] + x_i[j]) / 2.0 if u[j] < lb[j] else (ub[j] + x_i[j]) / 2.0\\n                        else:\\n                            u[j] = rng.uniform(lb[j], ub[j])\\n\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, u)\\n                evals += 1\\n\\n                # Greedy selection with success-history update\\n                if trial_f <= fitness[i]:\\n                    new_pop[i] = u\\n                    new_fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    S_F.append(F_i)\\n                    S_CR.append(CR_i)\\n                    S_delta.append(abs(fitness[i] - trial_f) + 1e-12)\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = u.copy()\\n                    # If the budget was exhausted inside the generation, keep the old\\n            # individuals for the remaining slots.\\n            for i in range(current_pop_size):\\n                if new_fitness[i] == np.inf and fitness[i] != np.inf:\\n                    new_pop[i] = pop[i]\\n                    new_fitness[i] = fitness[i]\\n\\n            pop = new_pop.copy()\\n            fitness = new_fitness.copy()\\n\\n            # This is\n     5\tthe hook that a DQN-based controller should implement.\n     6\t\"\"\"\n     7\tfrom abc import ABC, abstractmethod\n     8\tfrom typing import Dict, Any\n     9\t\n    10\timport numpy as np\n    11\t\n    12\t# Import global configuration\n    13\timport sys\n    14\timport os\n    15\ttry:\n    16\t    sys.path.insert(0, '/hada')\n    17\t    from config import set_global_seed, SEED\n    18\t    # Set global seed at module load time\n    19\t    set_global_seed()\n    20\texcept Exception:\n    21\t    # Fallback if config module is not available\n    22\t    SEED = 42\n    23\t    def set_global_seed(*args, **kwargs):\n    24\t        pass\n    25\t\n    26\t\n    27\tclass ParamController(ABC):\n    28\t    @abstractmethod\n    29\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    30\t        \"\"\"Called at the start of optimization for a new problem/run.\"\"\"\n    31\t\n    32\t    @abstractmethod\n    33\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    34\t        \"\"\"Given an observation, return a dict of PSO parameters to use.\"\"\"\n    35\t\n    36\t\n    37\tclass StaticController(ParamController):\n    38\t    def __init__(self, params: Dict[str, float]):\n    39\t        self.params = dict(params)\n    40\t\n    41\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    42\t        return None\n    43\t\n    44\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    45\t        return dict(self.params)\n    46\t\n    47\t\n    48\tclass DummyHeuristicController(ParamController):\n    49\t    \"\"\"A tiny heuristic that decays inertia weight and adjusts\n    50\t    cognitive/social coefficients based on progress.\"\"\"\n    51\t    def __init__(self, w_start=0.9, w_end=0.4, decay_iters=100):\n    52\t        self.w_start = w_start\n    53\t        self.w_end = w_end\n    54\t        self.decay_iters = decay_iters\n    55\t\n    56\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    57\t        self.it = 0\n    58\t\n    59\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    60\t        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n    61\t        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max(1, self.decay_iters))\n    62\t        w = self.w_start * (1 - t) + self.w_end * t\n    63\t        # Encourage exploration early, exploitation later\n    64\t        c1 = 1.8 * (1.0 - 0.5 * t)\n    65\t        c2 = 1.4 + 0.4 * t\n    66\t        # Also support DE-style parameters for multi-objective DE optimizers\n    67\t        F = 0.9 - 0.5 * t  # Large mutation early, refined late\n    68\t        CR = 0.9 - 0.4 * t # More crossover early for diversity\n    69\t        return {'w': w, 'c1': c1, 'c2': c2, 'F': F, 'CR': CR}\n    70\t\n    71\t\n    72\tclass AdaptiveDEController(ParamController):\n    73\t    \"\"\"Adaptive controller for DE/JADE algorithms with epsilon-constrained support.\n    74\t\n    75\t    - Early iterations: high F (exploration), high CR (diversity),\n    76\t      use 'rand/1' strategy to explore broadly.\n    77\t    - Late iterations: lower F (exploitation), moderate CR (retain\n    78\t      good building blocks), switch to 'jade' for fast convergence.\n    79\t    - Uses stagnation detection to temporarily boost F/CR.\n    80\t    - Provides a decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained handling,\n    81\t      allowing the optimizer to explore mildly infeasible regions early and\n    82\t      enforcing feasibility as the budget is consumed.\n    83\t    - If constraint violations are still large late in the run, epsilon is kept\n    84\t      nonzero to avoid discarding useful search directions prematurely.\n    85\t    \"\"\"\n    86\t    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.3, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.4,\n    87\t                 strategy_change_iter=0.3, stagnation_boost=0.2,\n    88\t                 epsilon_start=1e-2, epsilon_end=1e-8):\n    89\t        self.f_start = f_start\n    90\t        self.f_end = f_end\n    91\t        self.cr_start = cr_start\n    92\t        self.cr_end = cr_end\n    93\t        self.strategy_change_iter = strategy_change_iter\n    94\t        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\n    95\t        self.epsilon_start = epsilon_start\n    96\t        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n    97\t\n    98\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    99\t        self.it = 0\n   100\t        self.last_gbest = None\n   101\t        self.last_gbest_cv = None\n   102\t        self.stagnation_count = 0\n   103\t\n   104\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   105\t        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n   106\t        max_iter = max(1, int(observation.get('max_evals', 10000) / 100))\n   107\t        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max_iter)\n   108\t\n   109\t        # Smooth progress-based decay from exploration to exploitation\n   110\t        F = self.f_start * (1 - t) + self.f_end * t\n   111\t        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\n   112\t\n   113\t        # Detect stagnation: no improvement in best fitness for several iterations\n   114\t        current_gbest = observation.get('gbest_f', None)\n   115\t        if current_gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\n   116\t            if current_gbest < self.last_gbest - 1e-12:\n   117\t                self.stagnation_count = 0\n   118\t            else:\n   119\t                self.stagnation_count += 1\n   120\t        self.last_gbest = current_gbest\n   121\t\n   122\t        # If stagnated for a while, add extra diversity\n   123\t        if self.stagnation_count > 5:\n   124\t            F = min(1.0, F + self.stagnation_boost)\n   125\t            CR = min(1.0, CR + self.stagnation_boost)\n   126\t            self.stagnation_count = 0\n   127\t\n   128\t        # Switch strategy: rand/1 early for exploration, jade later for convergence\n   129\t        if t < self.strategy_change_iter:\n   130\t            strategy = 'rand/1'\n   131\t        else:\n   132\t            strategy = 'jade'\n   133\t\n   134\t        # Decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained DE.\n   135\t        # Use log-scale interpolation for smooth transition.\n   136\t        log_eps_start = float(np_log(self.epsilon_start))\n   137\t        log_eps_end = float(np_log(self.epsilon_end))\n   138\t        log_eps = log_eps_start + t * (log_eps_end - log_eps_start)\n   139\t        epsilon = float(np_exp(log_eps))\n   140\t\n   141\t        # If the current best is still infeasible late in the run, relax epsilon\n   142\t        # slightly so the algorithm is not forced into a tiny feasible region.\n   143\t        current_gbest_cv = observation.get('gbest_cv', None)\n   144\t        if current_gbest_cv is not None and t > 0.5 and current_gbest_cv > epsilon:\n   145\t            epsilon = max(epsilon, min(current_gbest_cv * 0.5, self.epsilon_start))\n   146\t\n   147\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR), 'strategy': strategy,\n   148\t                'epsilon': float(epsilon)}\n\"\n    }\n    </json>",

It can load/save a model\nand be used at inference time inside the DE optimizer.\n\"\"\"\nfrom typing import Dict, Any\nimport os\nimport numpy as np\ntry:\n    import torch\n    import torch.nn as nn\n    import torch.nn.functional as F\nexcept Exception:\n    torch = None\n\nfrom param_controller import ParamController\n\nimport sys\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\nfrom config import SEED, set_global_seed\n\nif torch is not None:\n    class _Net(nn.Module):\n        def __init__(self, obs_dim, action_dim, seed=None):\n            super().__init__()\n            self.fc1 = nn.Linear(obs_dim, 128)\n            self.fc2 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n            self.fc3 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n            self.out = nn.Linear(128, action_dim)\n            \n            # \u5982\u679c\u63d0\u4f9b\u4e86\u79cd\u5b50\uff0c\u4f7f\u7528\u5b83\u6765\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u6743\u91cd\n            if seed is not None:\n                self._init_weights(seed)\n\n        def _init_weights(self, seed):\n            \"\"\"\u4f7f\u7528\u56fa\u5b9a\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\u6743\u91cd\"\"\"\n            g = torch.Generator()\n            g.manual_seed(seed)\n            \n            # \u4f7f\u7528Kaiming\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n            for m in [self.fc1, self.fc2, self.fc3, self.out]:\n                torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(m.weight, nonlinearity='leaky_relu')\n                torch.nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)\n\n        def forward(self, x):\n            x = x.type_as(self.fc1.weight)\n            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc1(x), 0.2)\n            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc2(x), 0.2)\n            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc3(x), 0.2)\n            return self.out(x)\n\n\nclass DQNController(ParamController):\n    \"\"\"Simple continuous-action DQN controller for DE parameters.\n\n    The network outputs unbounded real values; `step` maps them to\n    DE parameter ranges: F in [0.1, 1.0], CR in [0.0, 1.0].\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, device: str = 'cpu'):\n        if torch is None:\n            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DQNController')\n        self.device = torch.device(device)\n        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n        self.action_dim = 2  # F, CR\n        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n            self.load(model_path)\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        return None\n\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n        # normalized progress and relative improvement\n        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n        else:\n            rel_improve = 0.0\n        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n        \n        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n        \n        with torch.no_grad():\n            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n            try:\n                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n            except Exception:\n                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n\n        out = out_t.numpy()\n\n        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n        try:\n            f_raw = float(out[0])\n            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n        except Exception:\n            f_sig = 0.5\n        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n        \n        # Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n        try:\n            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n        except Exception:\n            cr_sig = 0.9\n        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n        \n        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR)}\n\n    def save(self, path: str):\n        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n\n    def load(self, path: str):\n        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\n\nclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n\n    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n    The network outputs Q-values for each discrete action; `step`\n    returns the greedy action as a dict (matching ParamController).\n\n    Online training: stores transitions in a replay buffer and performs\n    a DQN update every `update_freq` steps.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, bins: int = 5,\n                device: str = 'cpu', training: bool = False, update_freq: int = 10,\n                buffer_capacity: int = 1000, batch_size: int = 64,\n                lr: float = 1e-4, gamma: float = 0.99, target_update_freq: int = 50,\n                max_grad_norm: float = 1.0, epsilon_start: float = 1.0,\n                epsilon_end: float = 0.05, epsilon_decay: float = 0.999,\n                seed: int = None):\n        if torch is None:\n            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DiscreteDQNController')\n        self.device = torch.device(device)\n        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n        self.bins = int(bins)\n        self.seed = seed if seed is not None else 42\n        \n        # \u8bbe\u7f6ePyTorch\u79cd\u5b50\u786e\u4fdd\u7f51\u7edc\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u53ef\u590d\u73b0\n        torch.manual_seed(self.seed)\n        if torch.cuda.is_available():\n            torch.cuda.manual_seed(self.seed)\n            torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(self.seed)\n        \n        # \u786e\u4fddPyTorch\u64cd\u4f5c\u5b8c\u5168\u786e\u5b9a\u6027\n        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True\n        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False\n        torch.backends.cudnn.enabled = False  # \u7981\u7528cudnn\u4ee5\u5b8c\u5168\u6d88\u9664\u968f\u673a\u6027\n        \n        self.rng = np.random.RandomState(self.seed)\n\n        # DE parameter discretization\n        self.F_vals = np.linspace(0.1, 1.0, self.bins)\n        self.CR_vals = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, self.bins)\n\n        self.actions = []\n        for fi in self.F_vals:\n            for cri in self.CR_vals:\n                self.actions.append({'F': float(fi), 'CR': float(cri)})\n        self.n_actions = len(self.actions)\n        \n        # \u4f7f\u7528\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\n        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.n_actions, seed=self.seed).to(self.device)\n\n        # Training setup\n        self.training = training\n        self.update_freq = update_freq\n        self.batch_size = batch_size\n        self.gamma = gamma\n        self.target_update_freq = target_update_freq\n        self.step_count = 0\n        self.total_loss = 0.0\n        self.total_reward = 0.0\n        self.loss_history = []\n        self.reward_history = []\n        self.return_record_interval = 5\n        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n\n        self.max_grad_norm = max_grad_norm\n        self.epsilon = epsilon_start\n        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n        self.epsilon_decay = epsilon_decay\n        if training:\n            # target_net\u4e5f\u9700\u8981\u4f7f\u7528\u76f8\u540c\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n            self.target_net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.n_actions, seed=self.seed).to(self.device)\n            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n            self.optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(self.net.parameters(), lr=lr)\n            self.buffer_capacity = buffer_capacity\n            self.replay_buffer = []\n            self.buffer_pos = 0\n\n        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n            try:\n                self.load(model_path)\n            except Exception:\n                pass\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        return None\n\n    def _obs_to_tensor(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> torch.Tensor:\n        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\"\n    }\n    </json>",

It can load/save a model\nand be used at inference time inside the DE optimizer.\n\"\"\"\nfrom typing import Dict, Any\nimport os\nimport numpy as np\ntry:\n    import torch\n    import torch.nn as nn\n    import torch.nn.functional as F\nexcept Exception:\n    torch = None\n\nfrom param_controller import ParamController\n\nimport sys\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\nfrom config import SEED, set_global_seed\n\nif torch is not None:\n    class _Net(nn.Module):\n        def __init__(self, obs_dim, action_dim, seed=None):\n            super().__init__()\n            self.fc1 = nn.Linear(obs_dim, 128)\n            self.fc2 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n            self.fc3 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n            self.out = nn.Linear(128, action_dim)\n            \n            # \u5982\u679c\u63d0\u4f9b\u4e86\u79cd\u5b50\uff0c\u4f7f\u7528\u5b83\u6765\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u6743\u91cd\n            if seed is not None:\n                self._init_weights(seed)\n\n        def _init_weights(self, seed):\n            \"\"\"\u4f7f\u7528\u56fa\u5b9a\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\u6743\u91cd\"\"\"\n            g = torch.Generator()\n            g.manual_seed(seed)\n            \n            # \u4f7f\u7528Kaiming\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n            for m in [self.fc1, self.fc2, self.fc3, self.out]:\n                torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(m.weight, nonlinearity='leaky_relu')\n                torch.nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)\n\n        def forward(self, x):\n            x = x.type_as(self.fc1.weight)\n            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc1(x), 0.2)\n            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc2(x), 0.2)\n            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc3(x), 0.2)\n            return self.out(x)\n\n\nclass DQNController(ParamController):\n    \"\"\"Simple continuous-action DQN controller for DE parameters.\n\n    The network outputs unbounded real values; `step` maps them to\n    DE parameter ranges: F in [0.1, 1.0], CR in [0.0, 1.0].\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, device: str = 'cpu'):\n        if torch is None:\n            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DQNController')\n        self.device = torch.device(device)\n        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n        self.action_dim = 2  # F, CR\n        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n            self.load(model_path)\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        return None\n\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n        # normalized progress and relative improvement\n        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n        else:\n            rel_improve = 0.0\n        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n        \n        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n        \n        with torch.no_grad():\n            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n            try:\n                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n            except Exception:\n                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n\n        out = out_t.numpy()\n\n        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n        try:\n            f_raw = float(out[0])\n            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n        except Exception:\n            f_sig = 0.5\n        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n        \n        # Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n        try:\n            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n        except Exception:\n            cr_sig = 0.9\n        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n        \n        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR)}\n\n    def save(self, path: str):\n        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n\n    def load(self, path: str):\n        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\n\nclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n\n    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n    The network outputs Q-values for each discrete action; `step`\n    returns the greedy action as a dict (matching ParamController).\n\n    Online training: stores transitions in a replay buffer and performs\n    a DQN update every `update_freq` steps.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, bins: int = 5,\n                device: str = 'cpu', training: bool = False, update_freq: int = 10,\n                buffer_capacity: int = 1000, batch_size: int = 64,\n                lr: float = 1e-4, gamma: float = 0.99, target_update_freq: int = 50,\n                max_grad_norm: float = 1.0, epsilon_start: float = 1.0,\n                epsilon_end: float = 0.05, epsilon_decay: float = 0.999,\n                seed: int = None):\n        if torch is None:\n            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DiscreteDQNController')\n        self.device = torch.device(device)\n        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n        self.bins = int(bins)\n        self.seed = seed if seed is not None else 42\n        \n        # \u8bbe\u7f6ePyTorch\u79cd\u5b50\u786e\u4fdd\u7f51\u7edc\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u53ef\u590d\u73b0\n        torch.manual_seed(self.seed)\n        if torch.cuda.is_available():\n            torch.cuda.manual_seed(self.seed)\n            torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(self.seed)\n        \n        # \u786e\u4fddPyTorch\u64cd\u4f5c\u5b8c\u5168\u786e\u5b9a\u6027\n        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True\n        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False\n        torch.backends.cudnn.enabled = False  # \u7981\u7528cudnn\u4ee5\u5b8c\u5168\u6d88\u9664\u968f\u673a\u6027\n        \n        self.rng = np.random.RandomState(self.seed)\n\n        # DE parameter discretization\n        self.F_vals = np.linspace(0.1, 1.0, self.bins)\n        self.CR_vals = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, self.bins)\n\n        self.actions = []\n        for fi in self.F_vals:\n            for cri in self.CR_vals:\n                self.actions.append({'F': float(fi), 'CR': float(cri)})\n        self.n_actions = len(self.actions)\n        \n        # \u4f7f\u7528\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\n        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.n_actions, seed=self.seed).to(self.device)\n\n        # Training setup\n        self.training = training\n        self.update_freq = update_freq\n        self.batch_size = batch_size\n        self.gamma = gamma\n        self.target_update_freq = target_update_freq\n        self.step_count = 0\n        self.total_loss = 0.0\n        self.total_reward = 0.0\n        self.loss_history = []\n        self.reward_history = []\n        self.return_record_interval = 5\n        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n\n        self.max_grad_norm = max_grad_norm\n        self.epsilon = epsilon_start\n        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n        self.epsilon_decay = epsilon_decay\n        if training:\n            # target_net\u4e5f\u9700\u8981\u4f7f\u7528\u76f8\u540c\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n            self.target_net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.n_actions, seed=self.seed).to(self.device)\n            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n            self.optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(self.net.parameters(), lr=lr)\n            self.buffer_capacity = buffer_capacity\n            self.replay_buffer = []\n            self.buffer_pos = 0\n\n        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n            try:\n                self.load(model_path)\n            except Exception:\n                pass\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        return None\n\n    def _obs_to_tensor(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> torch.Tensor:\n        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\"\n    }\n    </json>"

This is\nthe hook that a DQN-based controller should implement.\n\"\"\"\nfrom abc import ABC, abstractmethod\nfrom typing import Dict, Any\n\nimport numpy as np\n\n# Import global configuration\nimport sys\nimport os\ntry:\n    sys.path.insert(0, '/hada')\n    from config import set_global_seed, SEED\n    # Set global seed at module load time\n    set_global_seed()\nexcept Exception:\n    # Fallback if config module is not available\n    SEED = 42\n    def set_global_seed(*args, **kwargs):\n        pass\n\n\nclass ParamController(ABC):\n    @abstractmethod\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Called at the start of optimization for a new problem/run.\"\"\"\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        \"\"\"Given an observation, return a dict of PSO parameters to use.\"\"\"\n\n\nclass StaticController(ParamController):\n    def __init__(self, params: Dict[str, float]):\n        self.params = dict(params)\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        return None\n\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        return dict(self.params)\n\n\nclass DummyHeuristicController(ParamController):\n    \"\"\"A tiny heuristic that decays inertia weight and adjusts\n    cognitive/social coefficients based on progress.\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, w_start=0.9, w_end=0.4, decay_iters=100):\n        self.w_start = w_start\n        self.w_end = w_end\n        self.decay_iters = decay_iters\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        self.it = 0\n\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max(1, self.decay_iters))\n        w = self.w_start * (1 - t) + self.w_end * t\n        # Encourage exploration early, exploitation later\n        c1 = 1.8 * (1.0 - 0.5 * t)\n        c2 = 1.4 + 0.4 * t\n        # Also support DE-style parameters for multi-objective DE optimizers\n        F = 0.9 - 0.5 * t  # Large mutation early, refined late\n        CR = 0.9 - 0.4 * t # More crossover early for diversity\n        # L-SHADE style parameters: larger pbest pool early for exploration,\n        # smaller later for convergence; modest archive for diversity.\n        pbest_ratio = 0.25 - 0.1 * t\n        archive_ratio = 2.0 + 0.5 * (1.0 - t)\n        return {'w': w, 'c1': c1, 'c2': c2, 'F': F, 'CR': CR,\n                'pbest_ratio': pbest_ratio, 'archive_ratio': archive_ratio}\n\n\nclass AdaptiveDEController(ParamController):\n    \"\"\"Adaptive controller for DE/JADE algorithms with epsilon-constrained support.\n\n    - Early iterations: high F (exploration), high CR (diversity),\n      use 'rand/1' strategy to explore broadly.\n    - Late iterations: lower F (exploitation), moderate CR (retain\n      good building blocks), switch to 'jade' for fast convergence.\n    - Uses stagnation detection to temporarily boost F/CR.\n    - Provides a decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained handling,\n      allowing the optimizer to explore mildly infeasible regions early and\n      enforcing feasibility as the budget is consumed.\n    - If constraint violations are still large late in the run, epsilon is kept\n      nonzero to avoid discarding useful search directions prematurely.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.3, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.4,\n                 strategy_change_iter=0.3, stagnation_boost=0.2,\n                 epsilon_start=1e-2, epsilon_end=1e-8):\n        self.f_start = f_start\n        self.f_end = f_end\n        self.cr_start = cr_start\n        self.cr_end = cr_end\n        self.strategy_change_iter = strategy_change_iter\n        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\n        self.epsilon_start = epsilon_start\n        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        self.it = 0\n        self.last_gbest = None\n        self.last_gbest_cv = None\n        self.stagnation_count = 0\n\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n        max_iter = max(1, int(observation.get('max_evals', 10000) / 100))\n        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max_iter)\n\n        # Smooth progress-based decay from exploration to exploitation\n        F = self.f_start * (1 - t) + self.f_end * t\n        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\n\n        # Detect stagnation: no improvement in best fitness for several iterations\n        current_gbest = observation.get('gbest_f', None)\n        if current_gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\n            if current_gbest < self.last_gbest - 1e-12:\n                self.stagnation_count = 0\n            else:\n                self.stagnation_count += 1\n        self.last_gbest = current_gbest\n\n        # If stagnated for a while, add extra diversity\n        if self.stagnation_count > 5:\n            F = min(1.0, F + self.stagnation_boost)\n            CR = min(1.0, CR + self.stagnation_boost)\n            self.stagnation_count = 0\n\n        # Switch strategy: rand/1 early for exploration, jade later for convergence\n        if t < self.strategy_change_iter:\n            strategy = 'rand/1'\n        else:\n            strategy = 'jade'\n\n        # Decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained DE.\n        # Use log-scale interpolation for smooth transition.\n        log_eps_start = float(np.log(self.epsilon_start))\n        log_eps_end = float(np.log(self.epsilon_end))\n        log_eps = log_eps_start + t * (log_eps_end - log_eps_start)\n        epsilon = float(np.exp(log_eps))\n\n        # If the current best is still infeasible late in the run, relax epsilon\n        # slightly so the algorithm is not forced into a tiny feasible region.\n        current_gbest_cv = observation.get('gbest_cv', None)\n        if current_gbest_cv is not None and t > 0.5 and current_gbest_cv > epsilon:\n            epsilon = max(epsilon, min(current_gbest_cv * 0.5, self.epsilon_start))\n\n        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR), 'strategy': strategy,\n                'epsilon': float(epsilon)}\"\n    }\n    </json>",

This is\nthe hook that a DQN-based controller should implement.\n\"\"\"\nfrom abc import ABC, abstractmethod\nfrom typing import Dict, Any\n\nimport numpy as np\n\n# Import global configuration\nimport sys\nimport os\ntry:\n    sys.path.insert(0, '/hada')\n    from config import set_global_seed, SEED\n    # Set global seed at module load time\n    set_global_seed()\nexcept Exception:\n    # Fallback if config module is not available\n    SEED = 42\n    def set_global_seed(*args, **kwargs):\n        pass\n\n\nclass ParamController(ABC):\n    @abstractmethod\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Called at the start of optimization for a new problem/run.\"\"\"\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        \"\"\"Given an observation, return a dict of PSO parameters to use.\"\"\"\n\n\nclass StaticController(ParamController):\n    def __init__(self, params: Dict[str, float]):\n        self.params = dict(params)\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        return None\n\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        return dict(self.params)\n\n\nclass DummyHeuristicController(ParamController):\n    \"\"\"A tiny heuristic that decays inertia weight and adjusts\n    cognitive/social coefficients based on progress.\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, w_start=0.9, w_end=0.4, decay_iters=100):\n        self.w_start = w_start\n        self.w_end = w_end\n        self.decay_iters = decay_iters\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        self.it = 0\n\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max(1, self.decay_iters))\n        w = self.w_start * (1 - t) + self.w_end * t\n        # Encourage exploration early, exploitation later\n        c1 = 1.8 * (1.0 - 0.5 * t)\n        c2 = 1.4 + 0.4 * t\n        # Also support DE-style parameters for multi-objective DE optimizers\n        F = 0.9 - 0.5 * t  # Large mutation early, refined late\n        CR = 0.9 - 0.4 * t # More crossover early for diversity\n        # L-SHADE style parameters: larger pbest pool early for exploration,\n        # smaller later for convergence; modest archive for diversity.\n        pbest_ratio = 0.25 - 0.1 * t\n        archive_ratio = 2.0 + 0.5 * (1.0 - t)\n        return {'w': w, 'c1': c1, 'c2': c2, 'F': F, 'CR': CR,\n                'pbest_ratio': pbest_ratio, 'archive_ratio': archive_ratio}\n\n\nclass AdaptiveDEController(ParamController):\n    \"\"\"Adaptive controller for DE/JADE algorithms with epsilon-constrained support.\n\n    - Early iterations: high F (exploration), high CR (diversity),\n      use 'rand/1' strategy to explore broadly.\n    - Late iterations: lower F (exploitation), moderate CR (retain\n      good building blocks), switch to 'jade' for fast convergence.\n    - Uses stagnation detection to temporarily boost F/CR.\n    - Provides a decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained handling,\n      allowing the optimizer to explore mildly infeasible regions early and\n      enforcing feasibility as the budget is consumed.\n    - If constraint violations are still large late in the run, epsilon is kept\n      nonzero to avoid discarding useful search directions prematurely.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.3, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.4,\n                 strategy_change_iter=0.3, stagnation_boost=0.2,\n                 epsilon_start=1e-2, epsilon_end=1e-8):\n        self.f_start = f_start\n        self.f_end = f_end\n        self.cr_start = cr_start\n        self.cr_end = cr_end\n        self.strategy_change_iter = strategy_change_iter\n        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\n        self.epsilon_start = epsilon_start\n        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        self.it = 0\n        self.last_gbest = None\n        self.last_gbest_cv = None\n        self.stagnation_count = 0\n\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n        max_iter = max(1, int(observation.get('max_evals', 10000) / 100))\n        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max_iter)\n\n        # Smooth progress-based decay from exploration to exploitation\n        F = self.f_start * (1 - t) + self.f_end * t\n        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\n\n        # Detect stagnation: no improvement in best fitness for several iterations\n        current_gbest = observation.get('gbest_f', None)\n        if current_gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\n            if current_gbest < self.last_gbest - 1e-12:\n                self.stagnation_count = 0\n            else:\n                self.stagnation_count += 1\n        self.last_gbest = current_gbest\n\n        # If stagnated for a while, add extra diversity\n        if self.stagnation_count > 5:\n            F = min(1.0, F + self.stagnation_boost)\n            CR = min(1.0, CR + self.stagnation_boost)\n            self.stagnation_count = 0\n\n        # Switch strategy: rand/1 early for exploration, jade later for convergence\n        if t < self.strategy_change_iter:\n            strategy = 'rand/1'\n        else:\n            strategy = 'jade'\n\n        # Decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained DE.\n        # Use log-scale interpolation for smooth transition.\n        log_eps_start = float(np.log(self.epsilon_start))\n        log_eps_end = float(np.log(self.epsilon_end))\n        log_eps = log_eps_start + t * (log_eps_end - log_eps_start)\n        epsilon = float(np.exp(log_eps))\n\n        # If the current best is still infeasible late in the run, relax epsilon\n        # slightly so the algorithm is not forced into a tiny feasible region.\n        current_gbest_cv = observation.get('gbest_cv', None)\n        if current_gbest_cv is not None and t > 0.5 and current_gbest_cv > epsilon:\n            epsilon = max(epsilon, min(current_gbest_cv * 0.5, self.epsilon_start))\n\n        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR), 'strategy': strategy,\n                'epsilon': float(epsilon)}\"\n    }\n    </json>"
