The algorithm accepts an optional `controller`\nobject implementing the ParamController interface to allow dynamic parameter\nadjustment (F, CR).\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple\nimport numpy as np\nimport os\nimport sys\n\n# Import SEED from config if available\n_config_seed = None\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\nfrom config import SEED as _config_seed\n\n\ndef evaluate_problem(problem, x):\n    \"\"\"Evaluate problem, handling both MetaBox (problem.func) and cocoex (problem(x)) styles.\"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(x, np.ndarray):\n        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    \n    if hasattr(problem, 'func'):\n        # MetaBox style: use problem.func(x), which REQUIRES 2D input [NP, dim]\n        if x.ndim == 1:\n            x = x.reshape(1, -1)\n        result = problem.func(x)\n        return float(np.mean(np.array(result).flatten()))\n    else:\n        # cocoex style: problem(x)\n        return float(problem(x))\n\n\nclass DEOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    \n    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during optimization.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, dim: int, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals: int,\n                 params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, controller=None, recorder=None,\n                 swarm_size: int = None):\n        self.dim = int(dim)\n        if lower_bounds is not None:\n            self.lower_bounds = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.lower_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.lower_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.lower_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.lower_bounds = -5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        if upper_bounds is not None:\n            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.upper_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.upper_bounds = 5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n        self.controller = controller\n        self.recorder = recorder\n        self.stats = {}\n        self.pop_size = swarm_size if swarm_size is not None else max(10, 5 * self.dim)\n        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n\n    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n        selected = []\n        for _ in range(tournsize):\n            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n            selected.append(idx)\n        best_idx = selected[0]\n        for idx in selected[1:]:\n            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n                best_idx = idx\n        return best_idx\n\n    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n        if self.controller is not None:\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n\n        # Use the configured swarm size (or a sensible default) as the initial\n        # population size for the L-SHADE style optimizer.\n        init_pop_size = max(4, int(self.pop_size))\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\n        \n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n\n        # L-SHADE / JADE control parameters\n        H = 10                     # success-history memory size\n        min_pop_size = max(4, min(init_pop_size, 4))\n\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (init_pop_size, self.dim))\n        \n        # Evaluate initial population\n        evals = 0\n        fitness = np.full(init_pop_size, np.inf)\n        \n        for i in range(init_pop_size):\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                break\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n            evals += 1\n\n        # Track best\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n        \n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n\n        # External archive for JADE/L-SHADE current-to-pbest mutation\n        archive = np.empty((0, self.dim))\n        archive_fitness = np.empty(0)\n\n        # Success-history memory for F and CR adaptation\n        M_F = np.full(H, 0.5)\n        M_CR = np.full(H, 0.5)\n        memory_index = 0\n\n        # Main loop\n        iteration = 0\n        done = False\n        \n        while not done:\n            iteration += 1\n            params = dict(self.params)\n            if self.controller is not None:\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n                    params.update(step_params)\n            \n            # Controller provides base F/CR. If absent, L-SHADE success-history\n            # adaptation is the default.\n            base_F = float(np.clip(params.get('F', 0.5), 1e-10, 1.0))\n            base_CR = float(np.clip(params.get('CR', 0.5), 0.0, 1.0))\n            p = float(np.clip(params.get('pbest_ratio', 0.15), 0.05, 0.5))\n            archive_size_factor = float(max(1.0, params.get('archive_ratio', 2.5)))\n\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n            current_pop_size = pop.shape[0]\n\n            new_pop = np.zeros_like(pop)\n            new_fitness = np.full(current_pop_size, np.inf)\n\n            # Indices of the p-best individuals, used in current-to-pbest mutation\n            p_best_count = max(2, int(current_pop_size * p))\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n            p_best_indices = set(sorted_idx[:p_best_count].tolist())\n\n            S_F = []\n            S_CR = []\n            S_delta = []\n\n            for i in range(current_pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n\n                # Generate F_i and CR_i from the success-history memory.\n                # Use controller-provided values occasionally for exploration of\n                # the parameter space.\n                if rng.random() < 0.1:\n                    F_i = base_F\n                    CR_i = base_CR\n                else:\n                    r = int(rng.randint(0, H))\n                    F_i = float(np.clip(M_F[r] + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy(), 0.0, 1.0))\n                    CR_i = float(np.clip(M_CR[r] + 0.1 * rng.randn(), 0.0, 1.0))\n                    if F_i < 1e-8:\n                        F_i = float(base_F)\n\n                # Select a p-best individual distinct from the current one\n                if i in p_best_indices and len(p_best_indices) > 1:\n                    pbest_candidates = [idx for idx in p_best_indices if idx != i]\n                    pbest_idx = int(pbest_candidates[rng.randint(0, len(pbest_candidates))])\n                else:\n                    pbest_idx = int(sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, p_best_count)])\n\n                # Select r1 (distinct from i and pbest)\n                candidates_r1 = [idx for idx in range(current_pop_size) if idx != i and idx != pbest_idx]\n                if len(candidates_r1) == 0:\n                    r1 = i\n                else:\n                    r1 = int(candidates_r1[rng.randint(0, len(candidates_r1))])\n\n                # Select r2 from the population or the external archive\n                if archive.shape[0] > 0:\n                    combined_pop = np.vstack([pop, archive])\n                    combined_fit = np.concatenate([fitness, archive_fitness])\n                else:\n                    combined_pop = pop\n                    combined_fit = fitness\n\n                available = [idx for idx in range(combined_pop.shape[0])\n                             if idx >= current_pop_size or (idx != i and idx != pbest_idx and idx != r1)]\n                if len(available) == 0:\n                    available = [i, pbest_idx, r1]\n                r2 = int(available[rng.randint(0, len(available))])\n\n                x_i = pop[i]\n                x_pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\n                x_r1 = pop[r1]\n                x_r2 = combined_pop[r2]\n\n                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\n                v = x_i + F_i * (x_pbest - x_i) + F_i * (x_r1 - x_r2)\n\n                # Binomial crossover\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n                u = np.copy(x_i)\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if rng.random() < CR_i or j == j_rand:\n                        u[j] = v[j]\n\n                # Boundary handling: midpoint repair toward parent or random reset\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if u[j] < lb[j] or u[j] > ub[j]:\n                        if rng.random() < 0.5:\n                            u[j] = (lb[j] + x_i[j]) / 2.0 if u[j] < lb[j] else (ub[j] + x_i[j]) / 2.0\n                        else:\n                            u[j] = rng.uniform(lb[j], ub[j])\n\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, u)\n                evals += 1\n\n                # Greedy selection with success-history update\n                if trial_f <= fitness[i]:\n                    new_pop[i] = u\n                    new_fitness[i] = trial_f\n                    S_F.append(F_i)\n                    S_CR.append(CR_i)\n                    S_delta.append(abs(fitness[i] - trial_f) + 1e-12)\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\n                        gbest = u.copy()\n                    # Store the replaced parent in the external archive\n                    if archive.shape[0] < int(archive_size_factor * current_pop_size):\n                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i]])\n                        archive_fitness = np.append(archive_fitness, fitness[i])\n                else:\n                    new_pop[i] = pop[i]\n                    new_fitness[i] = fitness[i]\n\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n\n            # If the budget was exhausted inside the generation, keep the old\n            # individuals for the remaining slots.\n            for i in range(current_pop_size):\n                if new_fitness[i] == np.inf and fitness[i] != np.inf:\n                    new_pop[i] = pop[i]\n                    new_fitness[i] = fitness[i]\n\n            pop = new_pop.copy()\n            fitness = new_fitness.copy()\n\n            # L-SHADE linear population size reduction\n            if evals < self.max_evals:\n                remaining_frac = 1.0 - evals / self.max_evals\n                target_pop_size = int(round(min_pop_size + (init_pop_size - min_pop_size) * remaining_frac))\n                target_pop_size = max(min_pop_size, target_pop_size)\n                if target_pop_size < current_pop_size:\n                    keep_idx = np.argsort(fitness)[:target_pop_size]\n                    pop = pop[keep_idx].copy()\n                    fitness = fitness[keep_idx].copy()\n                    max_archive = int(archive_size_factor * pop.shape[0])\n                    if archive.shape[0] > max_archive:\n                        if max_archive > 0:\n                            archive = archive[-max_archive:].copy()\n                            archive_fitness = archive_fitness[-max_archive:].copy()\n                        else:\n                            archive = np.empty((0, self.dim))\n                            archive_fitness = np.empty(0)\n\n            # Update F/CR success-history memory using successful parameters\n            if len(S_F) > 0:\n                SF = np.asarray(S_F, dtype=float)\n                SCR = np.asarray(S_CR, dtype=float)\n                delta = np.asarray(S_delta, dtype=float)\n\n                weights = delta / np.sum(delta)\n                denom = np.sum(weights * SF)\n                if denom > 0:\n                    w_lehmer_F = float(np.sum(weights * SF * SF) / denom)\n                else:\n                    w_lehmer_F = float(np.mean(SF))\n                w_mean_CR = float(np.sum(weights * SCR))\n\n                M_F[memory_index] = float(np.clip(w_lehmer_F, 0.0, 1.0))\n                M_CR[memory_index] = float(np.clip(w_mean_CR, 0.0, 1.0))\n                memory_index = (memory_index + 1) % H\n\n            # Compute a more informative reward: relative fitness improvement\n            if prev_gbest_f > gbest_f + 1e-12:\n                improved = float((prev_gbest_f - gbest_f) / max(1.0, abs(prev_gbest_f)))\n            else:\n                improved = 0.0\n\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n            \n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n\n        self.stats = {\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n            'evals': int(evals),\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\n        }\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n    </json>",

x.ndim == 1:\n            x = x.reshape(1, -1)\n        result = problem.func(x)\n        return float(np.mean(np.array(result).flatten()))\n    else:\n        # cocoex style: problem(x)\n        return float(problem(x))\n\n\nclass DEOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    \n    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during optimization.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, dim: int, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals: int,\n                 params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, controller=None, recorder=None,\n                 swarm_size: int = None):\n        self.dim = int(dim)\n        if lower_bounds is not None:\n            self.lower_bounds = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.lower_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.lower_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.lower_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.lower_bounds = -5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        if upper_bounds is not None:\n            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.upper_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.upper_bounds = 5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n        self.controller = controller\n        self.recorder = recorder\n        self.stats = {}\n        self.pop_size = swarm_size if swarm_size is not None else max(10, 5 * self.dim)\n        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n\n    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n        selected = []\n        for _ in range(tournsize):\n            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n            selected.append(idx)\n        best_idx = selected[0]\n        for idx in selected[1:]:\n            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n                best_idx = idx\n        return best_idx\n\n    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n        if self.controller is not None:\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n\n        # Use the configured swarm size (or a sensible default) as the initial\n        # population size for the L-SHADE style optimizer.\n        init_pop_size = max(4, int(self.pop_size))\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\n        \n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n\n        # L-SHADE / JADE control parameters\n        H = 10                     # success-history memory size\n        min_pop_size = max(4, min(init_pop_size, 4))\n\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (init_pop_size, self.dim))\n        \n        # Evaluate initial population\n        evals = 0\n        fitness = np.full(init_pop_size, np.inf)\n        \n        for i in range(init_pop_size):\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                break\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n            evals += 1\n\n        # Track best\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n        \n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n\n        # External archive for JADE/L-SHADE current-to-pbest mutation\n        archive = np.empty((0, self.dim))\n        archive_fitness = np.empty(0)\n\n        # Success-history memory for F and CR adaptation\n        M_F = np.full(H, 0.5)\n        M_CR = np.full(H, 0.5)\n        memory_index = 0\n\n        # Main loop\n        iteration = 0\n        done = False\n        \n        while not done:\n            iteration += 1\n            params = dict(self.params)\n            if self.controller is not None:\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n                    params.update(step_params)\n            \n            # Controller provides base F/CR. If absent, L-SHADE success-history\n            # adaptation is the default.\n            base_F = float(np.clip(params.get('F', 0.5), 1e-10, 1.0))\n            base_CR = float(np.clip(params.get('CR', 0.5), 0.0, 1.0))\n            p = float(np.clip(params.get('pbest_ratio', 0.15), 0.05, 0.5))\n            archive_size_factor = float(max(1.0, params.get('archive_ratio', 2.5)))\n\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n            current_pop_size = pop.shape[0]\n\n            new_pop = np.zeros_like(pop)\n            new_fitness = np.full(current_pop_size, np.inf)\n\n            # Indices of the p-best individuals, used in current-to-pbest mutation\n            p_best_count = max(2, int(current_pop_size * p))\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n            p_best_indices = set(sorted_idx[:p_best_count].tolist())\n\n            S_F = []\n            S_CR = []\n            S_delta = []\n\n            for i in range(current_pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n\n                # Generate F_i and CR_i from the success-history memory.\n                # Use controller-provided values occasionally for exploration of\n                # the parameter space.\n                if rng.random() < 0.1:\n                    F_i = base_F\n                    CR_i = base_CR\n                else:\n                    r = int(rng.randint(0, H))\n                    F_i = float(np.clip(M_F[r] + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy(), 0.0, 1.0))\n                    CR_i = float(np.clip(M_CR[r] + 0.1 * rng.randn(), 0.0, 1.0))\n                    if F_i < 1e-8:\n                        F_i = float(base_F)\n\n                # Select a p-best individual distinct from the current one\n                if i in p_best_indices and len(p_best_indices) > 1:\n                    pbest_candidates = [idx for idx in p_best_indices if idx != i]\n                    pbest_idx = int(pbest_candidates[rng.randint(0, len(pbest_candidates))])\n                else:\n                    pbest_idx = int(sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, p_best_count)])\n\n                # Select r1 (distinct from i and pbest)\n                candidates_r1 = [idx for idx in range(current_pop_size) if idx != i and idx != pbest_idx]\n                if len(candidates_r1) == 0:\n                    r1 = i\n                else:\n                    r1 = int(candidates_r1[rng.randint(0, len(candidates_r1))])\n\n                # Select r2 from the population or the external archive\n                if archive.shape[0] > 0:\n                    combined_pop = np.vstack([pop, archive])\n                    combined_fit = np.concatenate([fitness, archive_fitness])\n                else:\n                    combined_pop = pop\n                    combined_fit = fitness\n\n                available = [idx for idx in range(combined_pop.shape[0])\n                             if idx >= current_pop_size or (idx != i and idx != pbest_idx and idx != r1)]\n                if len(available) == 0:\n                    available = [i, pbest_idx, r1]\n                r2 = int(available[rng.randint(0, len(available))])\n\n                x_i = pop[i]\n                x_pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\n                x_r1 = pop[r1]\n                x_r2 = combined_pop[r2]\n\n                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\n                v = x_i + F_i * (x_pbest - x_i) + F_i * (x_r1 - x_r2)\n\n                # Binomial crossover\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n                u = np.copy(x_i)\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if rng.random() < CR_i or j == j_rand:\n                        u[j] = v[j]\n\n                # Boundary handling: midpoint repair toward parent or random reset\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if u[j] < lb[j] or u[j] > ub[j]:\n                        if rng.random() < 0.5:\n                            u[j] = (lb[j] + x_i[j]) / 2.0 if u[j] < lb[j] else (ub[j] + x_i[j]) / 2.0\n                        else:\n                            u[j] = rng.uniform(lb[j], ub[j])\n\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, u)\n                evals += 1\n\n                # Greedy selection with success-history update\n                if trial_f <= fitness[i]:\n                    new_pop[i] = u\n                    new_fitness[i] = trial_f\n                    S_F.append(F_i)\n                    S_CR.append(CR_i)\n                    S_delta.append(abs(fitness[i] - trial_f) + 1e-12)\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\n                        gbest = u.copy()\n                    # Store the replaced parent in the external archive\n                    if archive.shape[0] < int(archive_size_factor * current_pop_size):\n                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i]])\n                        archive_fitness = np.append(archive_fitness, fitness[i])\n                else:\n                    new_pop[i] = pop[i]\n                    new_fitness[i] = fitness[i]\n\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n\n            # If the budget was exhausted inside the generation, keep the old\n            # individuals for the remaining slots.\n            for i in range(current_pop_size):\n                if new_fitness[i] == np.inf and fitness[i] != np.inf:\n                    new_pop[i] = pop[i]\n                    new_fitness[i] = fitness[i]\n\n            pop = new_pop.copy()\n            fitness = new_fitness.copy()\n\n            # L-SHADE linear population size reduction\n            if evals < self.max_evals:\n                remaining_frac = 1.0 - evals / self.max_evals\n                target_pop_size = int(round(min_pop_size + (init_pop_size - min_pop_size) * remaining_frac))\n                target_pop_size = max(min_pop_size, target_pop_size)\n                if target_pop_size < current_pop_size:\n                    keep_idx = np.argsort(fitness)[:target_pop_size]\n                    pop = pop[keep_idx].copy()\n                    fitness = fitness[keep_idx].copy()\n                    max_archive = int(archive_size_factor * pop.shape[0])\n                    if archive.shape[0] > max_archive:\n                        if max_archive > 0:\n                            archive = archive[-max_archive:].copy()\n                            archive_fitness = archive_fitness[-max_archive:].copy()\n                        else:\n                            archive = np.empty((0, self.dim))\n                            archive_fitness = np.empty(0)\n\n            # Update F/CR success-history memory using successful parameters\n            if len(S_F) > 0:\n                SF = np.asarray(S_F, dtype=float)\n                SCR = np.asarray(S_CR, dtype=float)\n                delta = np.asarray(S_delta, dtype=float)\n\n                weights = delta / np.sum(delta)\n                denom = np.sum(weights * SF)\n                if denom > 0:\n                    w_lehmer_F = float(np.sum(weights * SF * SF) / denom)\n                else:\n                    w_lehmer_F = float(np.mean(SF))\n                w_mean_CR = float(np.sum(weights * SCR))\n\n                M_F[memory_index] = float(np.clip(w_lehmer_F, 0.0, 1.0))\n                M_CR[memory_index] = float(np.clip(w_mean_CR, 0.0, 1.0))\n                memory_index = (memory_index + 1) % H\n\n            # Compute a more informative reward: relative fitness improvement\n            if prev_gbest_f > gbest_f + 1e-12:\n                improved = float((prev_gbest_f - gbest_f) / max(1.0, abs(prev_gbest_f)))\n            else:\n                improved = 0.0\n\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n            \n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n\n        self.stats = {\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n            'evals': int(evals),\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\n        }\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n    </json>"

If `path` is a directory, `view` lists non-hidden files and directories up to 2 levels deep\\n* The `create` command cannot be used if the specified `path` already exists as a file\\n* If a `command` generates a long output, it will be truncated and marked with `<response clipped>`\\n* The `undo_edit` command will revert the last edit made to the file at `path`\\n\\nNotes for using the `str_replace` command:\\n* The `old_str` parameter should match EXACTLY one or more consecutive lines from the original file. If none is given, the full file is shown. If provided, the file will be shown in the indicated line number range, e.g. [11, 12] will show lines 11 and 12. Indexing at 1 to start. Setting `[start_line, -1]` shows all lines from `start_line` to the end of the file.', 'items': {'type': 'integer'}, 'type': 'array'}}, 'required': ['command', 'path']}}\n```\n\nYou can use tools in this format:\n<json>\n{\n    \"tool_name\": ...,\n    \"tool_input\": ...\n}\n</json>\n\nYou can use MULTIPLE tools in a single response by including multiple JSON blocks.\nSTRICTLY FOLLOW THE FORMAT OF TOOL_NAME AND TOOL_INPUT ABOVE.\nDO NOT HALLUCINATE OR MAKE UP ANYTHING.\n\n<json>\n    {\n        \"tool_name\": \"bash\",\n        \"tool_input\": {'command': \"wc -l /hada/metabbo/ec_algorithm.py && sed -n '120,320p' /hada/metabbo/ec_algorithm.py\"},\n        \"tool_output\": \"328 /hada/metabbo/ec_algorithm.py\n        archive = np.empty((0, self.dim))\n        archive_fitness = np.empty(0)\n\n        # Success-history memory for F and CR adaptation\n        M_F = np.full(H, 0.5)\n        M_CR = np.full(H, 0.5)\n        memory_index = 0\n\n        # Main loop\n        iteration = 0\n        done = False\n        \n        while not done:\n            iteration += 1\n            params = dict(self.params)\n            if self.controller is not None:\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n                    params.update(step_params)\n            \n            # Controller provides base F/CR. If absent, L-SHADE success-history\n            # adaptation is the default.\n            base_F = float(np.clip(params.get('F', 0.5), 1e-10, 1.0))\n            base_CR = float(np.clip(params.get('CR', 0.5), 0.0, 1.0))\n            p = float(np.clip(params.get('pbest_ratio', 0.15), 0.05, 0.5))\n            archive_size_factor = float(max(1.0, params.get('archive_ratio', 2.5)))\n\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n            current_pop_size = pop.shape[0]\n\n            new_pop = np.zeros_like(pop)\n            new_fitness = np.full(current_pop_size, np.inf)\n\n            # Indices of the p-best individuals, used in current-to-pbest mutation\n            p_best_count = max(2, int(current_pop_size * p))\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n            p_best_indices = set(sorted_idx[:p_best_count].tolist())\n\n            S_F = []\n            S_CR = []\n            S_delta = []\n\n            for i in range(current_pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n\n                # Generate F_i and CR_i from the success-history memory.\n                # Use controller-provided values occasionally for exploration of\n                # the parameter space.\n                if rng.random() < 0.1:\n                    F_i = base_F\n                    CR_i = base_CR\n                else:\n                    r = int(rng.randint(0, H))\n                    F_i = float(np.clip(M_F[r] + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy(), 0.0, 1.0))\n                    CR_i = float(np.clip(M_CR[r] + 0.1 * rng.randn(), 0.0, 1.0))\n                    if F_i < 1e-8:\n                        F_i = float(base_F)\n\n                # Select a p-best individual distinct from the current one\n                if i in p_best_indices and len(p_best_indices) > 1:\n                    pbest_candidates = [idx for idx in p_best_indices if idx != i]\n                    pbest_idx = int(pbest_candidates[rng.randint(0, len(pbest_candidates))])\n                else:\n                    pbest_idx = int(sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, p_best_count)])\n\n                # Select r1 (distinct from i and pbest)\n                candidates_r1 = [idx for idx in range(current_pop_size) if idx != i and idx != pbest_idx]\n                if len(candidates_r1) == 0:\n                    r1 = i\n                else:\n                    r1 = int(candidates_r1[rng.randint(0, len(candidates_r1))])\n\n                # Select r2 from the population or the external archive\n                if archive.shape[0] > 0:\n                    combined_pop = np.vstack([pop, archive])\n                    combined_fit = np.concatenate([fitness, archive_fitness])\n                else:\n                    combined_pop = pop\n                    combined_fit = fitness\n\n                available = [idx for idx in range(combined_pop.shape[0])\n                             if idx >= current_pop_size or (idx != i and idx != pbest_idx and idx != r1)]\n                if len(available) == 0:\n                    available = [i, pbest_idx, r1]\n                r2 = int(available[rng.randint(0, len(available))])\n\n                x_i = pop[i]\n                x_pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\n                x_r1 = pop[r1]\n                x_r2 = combined_pop[r2]\n\n                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\n                v = x_i + F_i * (x_pbest - x_i) + F_i * (x_r1 - x_r2)\n\n                # Binomial crossover\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n                u = np.copy(x_i)\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if rng.random() < CR_i or j == j_rand:\n                        u[j] = v[j]\n\n                # Boundary handling: midpoint repair toward parent or random reset\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if u[j] < lb[j] or u[j] > ub[j]:\n                        if rng.random() < 0.5:\n                            u[j] = (lb[j] + x_i[j]) / 2.0 if u[j] < lb[j] else (ub[j] + x_i[j]) / 2.0\n                        else:\n                            u[j] = rng.uniform(lb[j], ub[j])\n\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, u)\n                evals += 1\n\n                # Greedy selection with success-history update\n                if trial_f <= fitness[i]:\n                    new_pop[i] = u\n                    new_fitness[i] = trial_f\n                    S_F.append(F_i)\n                    S_CR.append(CR_i)\n                    S_delta.append(abs(fitness[i] - trial_f) + 1e-12)\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\n                        gbest = u.copy()\n                    # Store the replaced parent in the external archive\n                    if archive.shape[0] < int(archive_size_factor * current_pop_size):\n                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i]])\n                        archive_fitness = np.append(archive_fitness, fitness[i])\n                else:\n                    new_pop[i] = pop[i]\n                    new_fitness[i] = fitness[i]\n\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n\n            # If the budget was exhausted inside the generation, keep the old\n            # individuals for the remaining slots.\n            for i in range(current_pop_size):\n                if new_fitness[i] == np.inf and fitness[i] != np.inf:\n                    new_pop[i] = pop[i]\n                    new_fitness[i] = fitness[i]\n\n            pop = new_pop.copy()\n            fitness = new_fitness.copy()\n\n            # L-SHADE linear population size reduction\n            if evals < self.max_evals:\n                remaining_frac = 1.0 - evals / self.max_evals\n                target_pop_size = int(round(min_pop_size + (init_pop_size - min_pop_size) * remaining_frac))\n                target_pop_size = max(min_pop_size, target_pop_size)\n                if target_pop_size < current_pop_size:\n                    keep_idx = np.argsort(fitness)[:target_pop_size]\n                    pop = pop[keep_idx].copy()\n                    fitness = fitness[keep_idx].copy()\n                    max_archive = int(archive_size_factor * pop.shape[0])\n                    if archive.shape[0] > max_archive:\n                        if max_archive > 0:\n                            archive = archive[-max_archive:].copy()\n                            archive_fitness = archive_fitness[-max_archive:].copy()\n                        else:\n                            archive = np.empty((0, self.dim))\n                            archive_fitness = np.empty(0)\n\n            # Update F/CR success-history memory using successful parameters\n            if len(S_F) > 0:\n                SF = np.asarray(S_F, dtype=float)\n                SCR = np.asarray(S_CR, dtype=float)\n                delta = np.asarray(S_delta, dtype=float)\n\n                weights = delta / np.sum(delta)\n                denom = np.sum(weights * SF)\n                if denom > 0:\n                    w_lehmer_F = float(np.sum(weights * SF * SF) / denom)\n                else:\n                    w_lehmer_F = float(np.mean(SF))\n                w_mean_CR = float(np.sum(weights * SCR))\n\n                M_F[memory_index] = float(np.clip(w_lehmer_F, 0.0, 1.0))\n                M_CR[memory_index] = float(np.clip(w_mean_CR, 0.0, 1.0))\n                memory_index = (memory_index + 1) % H\n\n            # Compute a more informative reward: relative fitness improvement\n            if prev_gbest_f > gbest_f + 1e-12:\n                improved = float((prev_gbest_f - gbest_f) / max(1.0, abs(prev_gbest_f)))\n            else:\n                improved = 0.0\n\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n            \n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n\n        self.stats = {\"\n    }\n    </json>",
